const canvas = document.getElementById('gameCanvas'); const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); const controls = document.getElementById('controls'); const enlargeBtn = document.getElementById('enlargeBtn'); const skipBtn = document.getElementById('skipBtn'); const SCREEN_WIDTH = 1000; const SCREEN_HEIGHT = 750; let gameState = 'start'; // 'start', 'instruction', 'playing', 'finish' let currentLevel = 0; let afikomanX = 0; let afikomanY = 0; let afikomanSize = 70; let popAnimation = 0; const images = {}; const backgrounds = []; const sounds = {}; let logoImg = new Image(); let startAudioPlayed = false; function initAssets() { const afiImg = new Image(); afiImg.src = assets.images.afikoman; images.afikoman = afiImg; logoImg.src = assets.images.logo; assets.backgrounds.forEach((bgBase64) => { const img = new Image(); img.src = bgBase64; backgrounds.push(img); }); for (let key in assets.sounds) { sounds[key] = new Audio(assets.sounds[key]); } // Try to play start audio on first interaction if blocked window.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!startAudioPlayed && gameState === 'start') { playSound('start'); startAudioPlayed = true; } }, { once: false }); // Initial attempt setTimeout(() => { playSound('start'); startAudioPlayed = true; }, 500); } function playSound(key, callback) { if (sounds[key]) { sounds[key].currentTime = 0; if (callback) { sounds[key].onended = callback; } else { sounds[key].onended = null; } sounds[key].play().catch(e => { console.log("Audio play blocked", e); startAudioPlayed = false; }); } } function startLevel() { gameState = 'playing'; controls.style.display = 'flex'; afikomanSize = 70 - (currentLevel * 5); // Random position, avoiding edges and controls (left top 200x200 area) let margin = afikomanSize; afikomanX = margin + Math.random() * (SCREEN_WIDTH - 2 * margin); afikomanY = margin + Math.random() * (SCREEN_HEIGHT - 2 * margin); // Keep away from buttons if (afikomanX < 250 && afikomanY < 250) { afikomanX += 250; afikomanY += 250; } popAnimation = 0; } function nextLevel() { currentLevel++; if (currentLevel >= backgrounds.length) { gameState = 'finish'; controls.style.display = 'none'; playSound('finish'); } else { startLevel(); } } function drawStartScreen() { // Gradient background const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(SCREEN_WIDTH/2, SCREEN_HEIGHT/2, 100, SCREEN_WIDTH/2, SCREEN_HEIGHT/2, 600); grad.addColorStop(0, "#fff9c4"); grad.addColorStop(1, "#ffecb3"); ctx.fillStyle = grad; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT); if (logoImg.complete) { ctx.drawImage(logoImg, SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 150, 50, 300, 225); } ctx.font = "bold 70px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "#e65100"; ctx.shadowColor = "rgba(0,0,0,0.2)"; ctx.shadowBlur = 10; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.fillText("מצא את האפיקומן", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, 350); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; // Start button const btnX = SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 125; const btnY = 450; const btnW = 250; const btnH = 100; // Pulsing effect const pulse = Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.005) * 5; ctx.fillStyle = "#43a047"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(btnX - pulse, btnY - pulse, btnW + 2*pulse, btnH + 2*pulse, 20); ctx.fill(); ctx.font = "bold 50px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "white"; ctx.fillText("התחל", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, btnY + 65); } function drawInstructionScreen() { ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff"; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT); ctx.font = "bold 50px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "#333"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.fillText("חפשו את האפיקומן!", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, 150); if (images.afikoman.complete) { ctx.drawImage(images.afikoman, SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 75, SCREEN_HEIGHT / 2 - 75, 150, 150); } } function drawPlayingScreen() { if (backgrounds[currentLevel] && backgrounds[currentLevel].complete) { ctx.drawImage(backgrounds[currentLevel], 0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT); } // Draw logo during gameplay if (logoImg.complete) { ctx.drawImage(logoImg, SCREEN_WIDTH - 120, 20, 100, 75); } if (images.afikoman.complete) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(afikomanX, afikomanY); if (popAnimation > 0) { const scale = 1 + popAnimation * 2; ctx.scale(scale, scale); ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - popAnimation; } ctx.drawImage(images.afikoman, -afikomanSize / 2, -afikomanSize / 2, afikomanSize, afikomanSize); ctx.restore(); } } function drawFinishScreen() { ctx.fillStyle = "#e8f5e9"; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, SCREEN_WIDTH, SCREEN_HEIGHT); ctx.font = "bold 90px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "#2e7d32"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.fillText("כל הכבוד!", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, SCREEN_HEIGHT / 2 - 50); ctx.font = "45px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "#333"; ctx.fillText("מצאתם את כל האפיקומנים!", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, SCREEN_HEIGHT / 2 + 50); // Restart button const btnX = SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 125; const btnY = SCREEN_HEIGHT / 2 + 130; const btnW = 250; const btnH = 80; ctx.fillStyle = "#1e88e5"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(btnX, btnY, btnW, btnH, 15); ctx.fill(); ctx.font = "bold 40px Arial"; ctx.fillStyle = "white"; ctx.fillText("שחק שוב", SCREEN_WIDTH / 2, btnY + 55); } function update() { if (popAnimation > 0) { popAnimation += 0.08; if (popAnimation >= 1) { popAnimation = 0; nextLevel(); } } } function gameLoop() { update(); if (gameState === 'start') { drawStartScreen(); } else if (gameState === 'instruction') { drawInstructionScreen(); } else if (gameState === 'playing') { drawPlayingScreen(); } else if (gameState === 'finish') { drawFinishScreen(); } requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop); } canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); const mx = (e.clientX - rect.left) * (SCREEN_WIDTH / rect.width); const my = (e.clientY - rect.top) * (SCREEN_HEIGHT / rect.height); if (gameState === 'start') { // Check start button const btnX = SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 125; const btnY = 450; if (mx >= btnX && mx <= btnX + 250 && my >= btnY && my <= btnY + 100) { gameState = 'instruction'; playSound('instruction', () => { setTimeout(() => { startLevel(); }, 2000); }); } } else if (gameState === 'playing' && popAnimation === 0) { const dx = mx - afikomanX; const dy = my - afikomanY; const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy); if (dist < afikomanSize) { // Larger hit area for kids popAnimation = 0.01; playSound('good'); } } else if (gameState === 'finish') { // Check restart button const btnX = SCREEN_WIDTH / 2 - 125; const btnY = SCREEN_HEIGHT / 2 + 130; if (mx >= btnX && mx <= btnX + 250 && my >= btnY && my <= btnY + 80) { currentLevel = 0; gameState = 'start'; playSound('start'); } } }); enlargeBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); afikomanSize += 5; }); skipBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); playSound('try_again', () => { nextLevel(); }); }); initAssets(); gameLoop();
